Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in biochemistry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 386 and the invariant I(A,B) = 55 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the biochemistry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 386 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 55. QED.
